# Aggregated political leader scripted effects [S-Z], merged from per-country files.

# --- SAF_political_leaders.txt ---
SAF_assign_ANC_caucus_trait = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { ruling_party = 5 }
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_country_leader_with_trait = SAF_ANC_left_wing_caucus_trait
					has_country_leader_with_trait = SAF_ANC_right_wing_caucus_trait
				}
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = { add_country_leader_trait = SAF_ANC_left_wing_caucus_trait }
			50 = { add_country_leader_trait = SAF_ANC_right_wing_caucus_trait }
		}
	}
}

set_leader_SAF = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_saf_marthinus_van_schalkwyk
				picture = "marthinus_van_schalkwyk.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_saf_mmusi_maimane
				picture = "Western_Mmusi_Maimane.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_saf_tony_leon
				picture = "Portrait_Tony_Leon.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {

# --- SAM_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_SAM = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 23 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_sam_malietoa_tanumafili_ii
				picture = "Malietoa_Tanumafili_II.dds"
				ideology = Monarchist
				traits = {
					nationalist_Monarchist
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_sam_sualauvi_ii
				picture = "Sualauvi_II.dds"
				ideology = Monarchist
				traits = {
					nationalist_Monarchist
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# --- SAO_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_SAO = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 16 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Libertarian_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_Libertarian_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_sao_patrice_trovoada
				picture = "SAO_Patrice_Trovoada.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Libertarian
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_Libertarian
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_Libertarian_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# --- SAU_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_SAU = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 9 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Vilayat_e_Faqih_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Vilayat_e_Faqih_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_sau_abdelkarim_hussein_mohamed_al_nasser
				picture = "abdelkarim_hussein_mohamed_al_nasser.dds"
				ideology = Vilayat_e_Faqih
				traits = {
					emerging_Vilayat_e_Faqih
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Vilayat_e_Faqih_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Vilayat_e_Faqih_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Vilayat_e_Faqih_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_sau_abdelkarim_hussein_mohamed_al_nasser
				picture = "abdelkarim_hussein_mohamed_al_nasser.dds"
				ideology = Vilayat_e_Faqih
				traits = {
					emerging_Vilayat_e_Faqih
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Vilayat_e_Faqih_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 11 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Caliphate_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Caliphate_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_sey_david_pierre
				picture = "Portrait_David_Pierre.dds"
				ideology = neutral_autocracy
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { neutral_autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_sey_james_michel
				picture = "James_Michel.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { conservatism_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_sha_hamma_salama
				picture = "gfx/leaders/SHA/SHA_hamma_salama.dds"
				ideology = Nat_Autocracy
				traits = {
					nationalist_Nat_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
		}
	}
}

				}

		}

			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# --- SPR_political_leaders.txt ---
# Set Leader Guide
# Written by AngriestBird
# set_leader_<TAG> is the wrapper used in 99_election_effects.txt
set_leader_SPR = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { ruling_party = 23 } # set_<ideology_word>
			NOT = { # General conditionals to use any of these leaders in this group
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = SPR_chavy_deposition_of_monarchs_flag
					has_global_flag = SPR_french_execution_gangnam_flag
				}
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 0 } NOT = { has_country_flag = SPR_carlist_rising } } # Conditions to Trigger Leader 1
			add_to_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 } # You use this to increment to hit the next leader
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes } #Removes the current standing leader
			create_country_leader = { # Obvious
				name = SPR_juan_carlos_i
				picture = "juan_carlos_i.dds"
				ideology = Monarchist
				traits = {
					nationalist_Monarchist
					king
					military_career
				}
			}

			# Do Not Retire Condition: Setting this flag to keep the current leader. i.e. Juan Carlos is in power and I want to keep Juan Carlos I so I set this flag to prevent him from being replaced.
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 } }
			if = { # This sets a fail conditional if the leader is abdicated or if the date is under 2014
				limit = {
					OR = {
						date < 2014.1.1
						has_country_flag = SPR_abdication_of_juan_carlos
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
			} #skip if 2017
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 }
				NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = SPR_carlist_rising }
			}
			add_to_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = SPR_felipe_vi
				picture = "felipe_vi.dds"
				ideology = Monarchist
				traits = {
					nationalist_Monarchist
					king
					military_career
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 2 }
				NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } }
				has_country_flag = SPR_carlist_rising
			}
			add_to_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = SPR_carlos_hugo
				picture = "prince_charles_hugo.dds"
				ideology = Monarchist
				traits = {
					nationalist_Monarchist
					king
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						date < 2016.1.1
						has_country_flag = SPR_death_of_charles_hugo
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
			} #skip if 2017
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 3 }
				NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } }
				has_country_flag = SPR_carlist_rising
			}
			add_to_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = SPR_carlos_javier
				picture = "carlos_javier.dds"
				ideology = Monarchist
				traits = {
					nationalist_Monarchist
					king
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Monarchist_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = SPR_chavy_deposition_of_monarchs_flag
					has_global_flag = SPR_french_execution_gangnam_flag
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 0 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = SPR_carlist_rising }
			}
			add_to_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = SPR_juan_carlos_i
				picture = "juan_carlos_i.dds"
				ideology = Western_Autocracy
				traits = {
					western_Western_Autocracy
					king
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						date < 2016.1.1
						has_country_flag = SPR_abdication_of_juan_carlos
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
			} #skip if 2017
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
				NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = SPR_carlist_rising }
			}
			add_to_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = SPR_felipe_vi
				picture = "felipe_vi.dds"
				ideology = Western_Autocracy
				traits = {
					western_Western_Autocracy
					king
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
		# Carlists are listed as successors for now.
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 2 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }
			create_country_leader = {
				name = SPR_charles_hugo
				picture = "prince_charles_hugo.dds"
				ideology = Western_Autocracy
				traits = {
					western_Western_Autocracy
					king
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						date < 2016.1.1
						has_country_flag = SPR_abdication_of_juan_carlos
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
			} #skip if 2017
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}

set_leader_SSU = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_ssu_peter_abdurahman_sule
				picture = "peter_abdurahman.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism

set_leader_SWS = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 13 } }
        if = {
            limit = { date > 2018.12.18 }
            create_country_leader = {
                name = SWS_abdiaziz_hassan_mohamed_laftagareen
                picture = "gfx/leaders/generic_politicians/black_005.dds"
                ideology = Neutral_Autocracy
                traits = {
                    neutrality_Neutral_Autocracy
                }
            }
        }
        else = {
            create_country_leader = {
                name = SWS_sharif_hassan_sheikh_aden
                picture = "gfx/leaders/generic_politicians/black_002.dds"
                ideology = Neutral_Autocracy
                traits = {
                    neutrality_Neutral_Autocracy
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

set_leader_TUR = {
	if = { limit = { western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = TUR_fethullah_gulen

# --- UAE_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_UAE = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 3 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { socialism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_uae_nassar_bin_ghaith
				picture = "nassar_bin_ghaith.dds"
				ideology = socialism
				traits = {
					western_socialism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 11 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Caliphate_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Caliphate_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_uae_abd_al_rahim_al_nashiri
				picture = "Abd_Al-Rahim_Al-Nashiri.dds"
				ideology = Caliphate
				traits = {
					salafist_Caliphate
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Caliphate_leader = 1 } }
		}
	}
}

set_leader_USA = {

	###########################
	### Western Autocrats #####
	###########################
	if = { limit = { western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 0 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = USA_congressional_chambers
				picture = "Council_For_National_Preservation.dds"
				desc = "CONGRESSIONAL_CHAMBERS_DESC"
				ideology = Western_Autocracy
				traits = {
					western_Western_Autocracy
					emotional
					incapable

# --- UZB_political_leaders.txt ---
set_leader_UZB = {

	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 2 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { liberalism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = UZB_muhammad_salih
				picture = "muhammad_salih.dds"
				ideology = liberalism
				traits = {
					western_liberalism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { liberalism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 18 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { neutral_Social_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { neutral_Social_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = UZB_narimon_umarov
				picture = "narimon_umarov.dds"
				ideology = neutral_Social
				traits = {
					neutrality_neutral_Social
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { neutral_Social_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 22 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = UZB_qobul_berdiev
				picture = "Portrait_Qobul_Berdiyev.dds"
				ideology = Nat_Autocracy
				traits = {
					nationalist_Nat_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Nat_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { western_autocrats_are_in_power = yes }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = UZB_bakhodir_khan_turkistan
				picture = "bakhodir_khan.dds"
				ideology = Western_Autocracy
				traits = {
					western_Western_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Western_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 13 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = UZB_hotamzhon_ketmonov
				picture = "hotamzhon_ketmonov.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Autocracy
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_Autocracy
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_Autocracy_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 17 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_green_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_green_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = UZB_boriy_alikhanov
				picture = "boriy_alikhanov.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_green
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_green
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { Neutral_green_leader = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { b = 1 }
		}

set_leader_VEN = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 3 } }

				name = VEN_manuel_rosales

					western_socialism
				}
			}

		}

			add_to_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { socialism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_zam_levy_mwanawasa
				picture = "Levy_Mwanawasa.dds"
				expire = "2008.08.19"
				ideology = socialism
				traits = {
					western_socialism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_zam_rupiah_banda
				picture = "Rupiah_Banda.dds"
				expire = "2015.01.01"
				ideology = socialism
				traits = {
					western_socialism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { date < 2016.1.2 } set_temp_variable = { b = 1 } } #skip if 2017
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { socialism_leader = 2 } NOT = { check_variable = { b = 1 } } }
			add_to_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 }
			hidden_effect = { kill_country_leader = yes }

			create_country_leader = {
				name = ZZZ_nm_zam_nevers_mumba
				picture = "Nevers_Mumba.dds"
				ideology = socialism
				traits = {
					western_socialism
				}
			}

			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = do_not_retire } subtract_from_variable = { socialism_leader = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 5 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { anarchist_communism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { anarchist_communism_leader = 1 }

	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 6 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Conservative_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Conservative_leader = 1 }

	}
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 14 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_conservatism_leader = 1 }

	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 17 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_green_leader = 0 } }
			add_to_variable = { Neutral_green_leader = 1 }

	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 19 } }

		if = { limit = { check_variable = { Neutral_Communism_leader = 0 } }
